- **01_实际Skill**: 003e **编号**: SANSHENG-SKILL-24 --- name: lean-communication description: | 精益沟通技能 — 将五段式指令结构、意图契约、熔断机制固化为可复用的沟通协议。 适用于所有Egbertie↔满意姐/蓝军的交互，以及任何需要高效、低损耗、高置信度沟通的场景。 当用户发出指令、AI输出回应、任务开始/结束、需要确认理解时自动激活。 触发词："五段式" / "意图契约" / "阻塞条件" / "熔断" / "Token优化" / "精益" / "沟通协议" / "理解重述" / "确认" / "约束" --- ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D/%E4%B8%89%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%87%E7%89%A9%C2%B7%E4%BA%A7%E5%93%81%E5%8C%96%E7%BB%88%E7%82%BC/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97%C2%B7%E5%BB%B6%E4%BC%B8/24_%E7%B2%BE%E7%9B%8A%E6%B2%9F%E9%80%9A%E5%B7%A5%E7%A8%8B%E5%B8%88/01_%E5%AE%9E%E9%99%85Skill) · 2 copies)

- **01_实际Skill**: 003e **编号**: SANSHENG-SKILL-26 --- name: skill-cross-pollinator description: | 技能跨界重组技能 — 将任何技能的底层能力提取出来，映射到当前需求场景， 实现"Skill名称只是入口提示，不是使用边界"的无边界认知。 当用户面对新问题但不确定该用什么技能、当现有技能看似不匹配但底层能力可借用、 当需要创造性地组合多个技能时激活。 触发词："还有什么技能能用" / "这个能用吗" / "底层能力" / "跨界" / "无边界" / "重组" / "映射" / "skill还能怎么用" / "不要被名字限制" --- ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D/%E4%B8%89%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%87%E7%89%A9%C2%B7%E4%BA%A7%E5%93%81%E5%8C%96%E7%BB%88%E7%82%BC/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97%C2%B7%E5%BB%B6%E4%BC%B8/26_%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E8%B7%A8%E7%95%8C%E6%98%A0%E5%B0%84%E5%B8%88/01_%E5%AE%9E%E9%99%85Skill) · 2 copies)
- **01_实际Skill**: 003e **编号**: SANSHENG-SKILL-28 --- name: task-decomposition-artisan description: | 任务拆解技能 — 将复杂任务分解为可在3-5分钟内完成的最小可执行单元(Unit)， 梳理依赖关系，排序优先级。源自实战中反复验证的"最小单元"方法论。 当用户提出复杂任务、当任务预估超过10分钟、当需要多人/多阶段协作时激活。 触发词："拆解" / "拆分" / "分阶段" / "最小单元" / "U1" / "单元" / "怎么开始" / "太复杂" / "从哪下手" / "任务分解" --- ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D/%E4%B8%89%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%87%E7%89%A9%C2%B7%E4%BA%A7%E5%93%81%E5%8C%96%E7%BB%88%E7%82%BC/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97%C2%B7%E5%BB%B6%E4%BC%B8/28_%E4%BB%BB%E5%8A%A1%E6%8B%86%E8%A7%A3%E5%8C%A0/01_%E5%AE%9E%E9%99%85Skill) · 2 copies)
- **01_实际Skill**: 003e **编号**: SANSHENG-SKILL-25 --- name: rapid-cognitive-audit description: | 快速认知审计技能 — 蓝军10项认知审计清单的3分钟轻量版。 在任何判断、分析、建议输出前，强制发现至少1个问题，防止"一切正常"的幻觉。 源自蓝军Skeptor-7升级后的认知审计清单。 当AI即将输出判断/结论/建议、当用户要求验证某个观点、当置信度存疑时激活。 触发词："审计" / "批判" / "挑毛病" / "有什么问题" / "认知偏差" / "一切正常" / "置信度" / "快速审计" / "3分钟" / "Skeptor" --- ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D/%E4%B8%89%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%87%E7%89%A9%C2%B7%E4%BA%A7%E5%93%81%E5%8C%96%E7%BB%88%E7%82%BC/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97%C2%B7%E5%BB%B6%E4%BC%B8/25_%E5%BF%AB%E9%80%9F%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E5%AE%A1%E8%AE%A1/01_%E5%AE%9E%E9%99%85Skill) · 2 copies)

- **01_实际Skill**: 003e **编号**: SANSHENG-SKILL-21 --- name: dual-economy-auditor description: | 双经济审计技能 — 在每次行动、决策、产出前强制执行Token经济学与效益经济学双重检查。 这是满意解研究所的最高效力条款，不满足任一维度即拦截行动。 当用户要求执行任何任务、创建任何产出、消耗任何资源时自动激活。 触发词："双经济" / "审计" / "Token够吗" / "效益值得吗" / "检查点" / "向内集邮" / "创始人5分钟" / "本地优先" / "费用红线" --- ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D/%E4%B8%89%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%87%E7%89%A9%C2%B7%E4%BA%A7%E5%93%81%E5%8C%96%E7%BB%88%E7%82%BC/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97%C2%B7%E5%BB%B6%E4%BC%B8/21_%E5%8F%8C%E7%BB%8F%E6%B5%8E%E5%AE%A1%E8%AE%A1%E5%B8%88/01_%E5%AE%9E%E9%99%85Skill) · 2 copies)
- **01_实际Skill**: 003e **编号**: SANSHENG-SKILL-29 --- name: memory-sync-protocol description: | 记忆同步技能 — 系统化执行C1-C6静默前置条件，确保跨会话记忆一致性。 在每次会话启动、长任务恢复、系统重启后、从休眠唤醒时强制激活。 防止"记忆断层"——即声称记得但实际上记忆未同步导致的决策偏差。 触发词："C1-C6" / "记忆同步" / "重启自检" / "静默前置条件" / "恢复" / "唤醒" / "MEMORY.md" / "指针同步" / "session启动" / "记忆断层" --- ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D/%E4%B8%89%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%87%E7%89%A9%C2%B7%E4%BA%A7%E5%93%81%E5%8C%96%E7%BB%88%E7%82%BC/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97%C2%B7%E5%BB%B6%E4%BC%B8/29_%E8%AE%B0%E5%BF%86%E5%90%8C%E6%AD%A5%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE/01_%E5%AE%9E%E9%99%85Skill) · 2 copies)
- **01_实际Skill**: 003e **编号**: SANSHENG-SKILL-27 --- name: checkpoint-archivist description: | 断点存档技能 — 将灾备永生协议的7层状态栈简化为日常可执行的轻量存档流程。 在每次会话结束、任务中断、系统休眠前，快速保存当前工作状态，确保重启后可恢复。 当用户说"我去忙了""先这样""休眠""暂停"、当长任务需要中途保存、 当系统即将进入静默模式时强制激活。 触发词："存档" / "保存状态" / "休眠" / "暂停" / "断点" / "checkpoint" / "我去忙了" / "先这样" / "重启恢复" / "恢复工作" --- ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D/%E4%B8%89%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%87%E7%89%A9%C2%B7%E4%BA%A7%E5%93%81%E5%8C%96%E7%BB%88%E7%82%BC/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97%C2%B7%E5%BB%B6%E4%BC%B8/27_%E6%96%AD%E7%82%B9%E5%AD%98%E6%A1%A3%E5%B7%A5%E7%A8%8B%E5%B8%88/01_%E5%AE%9E%E9%99%85Skill) · 2 copies)

- **01_实际Skill**: 003e **编号**: SANSHENG-SKILL-30 --- name: decision-checkpoint description: | 决策断点存档技能 — 将编号决策响应协议V1.1固化为系统化的决策记录与恢复机制。 确保每个决策有编号、有选项、有记录、有追溯链。防止"我们之前决定过什么？"的记忆断层。 当呈现选项时、当用户做出选择时、当需要回顾历史决策时激活。 触发词："决策" / "选哪个" / "编号" / "DEC-" / "选项" / "A-①" / "B-②" / "我们之前决定" / "回溯" / "决策记录" / "decision" --- ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/%E6%BB%A1%E6%84%8F%E5%A7%90%E5%92%8C%E6%89%A3%E5%AD%90%E7%9A%84%E8%B5%84%E6%96%99/kimi-workspace%E5%AE%8C%E6%95%B4%E7%89%88/A-manyige/%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D/%E4%B8%89%E7%94%9F%E4%B8%87%E7%89%A9%C2%B7%E4%BA%A7%E5%93%81%E5%8C%96%E7%BB%88%E7%82%BC/%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97%C2%B7%E5%BB%B6%E4%BC%B8/30_%E5%86%B3%E7%AD%96%E6%96%AD%E7%82%B9%E5%AD%98%E6%A1%A3/01_%E5%AE%9E%E9%99%85Skill) · 2 copies)

- **01a-code-cartographer**: Parses Java source files and pom.xml across every Maven module in this workspace into an AST-derived artifacts.json (packages, classes, interfaces, methods, fields, annotations, REST mappings, Maven dependencies). Use when asked to scan/analyze the codebase, extract code structure, list classes/endpoints, or as the first step before building a Neo4j graph or architecture document. ([Udaradg/sample-java-project](https://github.com/Udaradg/sample-java-project/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/01a-code-cartographer) · 3 copies)
- **01a-code-cartographer**: Parse Java and Maven source into AST-derived architecture artifacts. Use to scan the codebase, list classes or endpoints, or prepare architecture and graph documentation. ([Vihanga22365/sample-java-project-with-artifacts](https://github.com/Vihanga22365/sample-java-project-with-artifacts/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/01a-code-cartographer) · 2 copies)
- **01a-gap-analysis**: Audits existing knowledge and identifies research gaps by scoring assumptions on confidence and impact. Use when starting research, identifying unknowns, or when new information introduces uncertainty. Triggers on: "what do we know", "gap analysis", "research gaps", "audit assumptions", "what don't we know". ([jrbrugger/design-workflow](https://github.com/jrbrugger/design-workflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-workflow/01-research/01a-gap-analysis) · ★1)
- **01a-select-scenario**: 指定シナリオID（おすすめ選択や明示指定で決まったID）があればそれを採用し、無ければフォールバックとして演習済みシナリオIDを除外して未演習シナリオを無作為順に並べ、今回の主シナリオと内部状況ファイルを作るためのskill。 ([Kuniwak/ttx](https://github.com/Kuniwak/ttx/tree/HEAD/.ai/skills/01a-select-scenario) · ★8)
- **01b-context-weaver**: Adds the semantic layer to the code knowledge graph. Selects the architecturally significant nodes (modules, packages, types, methods, endpoints, framework base types), briefs an author on each with real evidence, validates the resulting contextual descriptions against the schema and against artifacts.json, and serves them back to downstream agents. Use when asked to describe/document/annotate the graph, explain what parts of the code mean, add context for other agents, or as the step between Code Cartographer and Graph Forge. ([Udaradg/sample-java-project](https://github.com/Udaradg/sample-java-project/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/01b-context-weaver) · 3 copies)
- **01b-context-weaver**: Add evidence-backed semantic context to the architecture graph. Use to describe significant nodes, explain code meaning, or validate graph context after a source scan. ([Vihanga22365/sample-java-project-with-artifacts](https://github.com/Vihanga22365/sample-java-project-with-artifacts/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/01b-context-weaver) · 2 copies)
- **01b-list-scenarios**: 事前生成済みの SCENARIO-LIST.tsv を読み、シナリオIDと初動対応部署と最初の開示可能イベントを一覧表示するskill。所属部署キーワードが渡されれば、全体訓練では初動対応部署で、個別訓練では初動対応部署または受動対応部署で絞り込んでおすすめ候補を出す。シナリオ選択の判断材料に使う。表示する3列は開示可能情報のみなので参加者へ提示してもよい（受動対応部署列は表示しない）。 ([Kuniwak/ttx](https://github.com/Kuniwak/ttx/tree/HEAD/.ai/skills/01b-list-scenarios) · ★8)
- **01b-research-plan**: Structure and write UX research briefs with 11 sections, then auto-publish to Notion. Use when starting research, scoping a study, or writing a research brief. Triggers on: "research plan", "research brief", "plan research", "write a brief", "11-section brief". ([jrbrugger/design-workflow](https://github.com/jrbrugger/design-workflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-workflow/01-research/01b-research-plan) · ★1)
- **01c-competitive-analysis**: Conducts structured competitive analysis across direct competitors, adjacent products, and best-in-class references. Produces a populated competitive matrix, detailed UX profiles, opportunity map, and strategic positioning implications. Use at the start of any research or design phase, or when entering a new market. Triggers on: "competitive analysis", "competitors", "competitive landscape", "how does X compare", "what are competitors doing", "market landscape", "comp research". ([jrbrugger/design-workflow](https://github.com/jrbrugger/design-workflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-workflow/01-research/01c-competitive-analysis) · ★1)
- **01c-data-collection**: Executes research plans through interviews, analytics pulls, competitive analysis, and surveys. Use when conducting user interviews, competitive research, or gathering data. Triggers on: "interview guide", "competitive analysis", "collect data", "run interviews", "research session". ([jrbrugger/design-workflow](https://github.com/jrbrugger/design-workflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-workflow/01-research/01c-data-collection) · ★1)
- **01c-graph-forge**: Loads Code Cartographer artifacts.json into a Neo4j graph database, modeling Modules, Packages, Types, Methods, Endpoints and Maven dependencies with relationships (CONTAINS, EXTENDS, IMPLEMENTS, DEPENDS_ON, EXPOSES, USES, HAS_METHOD), and attaches the Context Weaver semantic layer (what each node is for, how it fails, what it touches) onto the same nodes. Use when asked to build/update a knowledge graph, load the code graph into Neo4j, or visualize/query code connections in Neo4j. ([Udaradg/sample-java-project](https://github.com/Udaradg/sample-java-project/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/01c-graph-forge) · 3 copies)
- **01c-graph-forge**: Load AST artifacts and semantic context into the Neo4j code knowledge graph. Use to build, refresh, query, or visualize the repository dependency graph. ([Vihanga22365/sample-java-project-with-artifacts](https://github.com/Vihanga22365/sample-java-project-with-artifacts/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/01c-graph-forge) · 2 copies)
- **01c-metrics**: Pull behavioral analytics data from Mixpanel, Amplitude, PostHog, or GA4 to answer specific research gaps with quantitative evidence. Translates plain-language research questions into API queries, synthesizes results as research evidence, and feeds findings into the insight report and gap tracker. Use when research gaps can be answered with behavioral data, or when triangulating qualitative interview findings with usage patterns. Triggers on: "analytics", "metrics", "Mixpanel", "Amplitude", "funnel data", "retention data", "usage data", "behavioral data", "quantitative research". ([jrbrugger/design-workflow](https://github.com/jrbrugger/design-workflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-workflow/01-research/01c-metrics) · ★1)
- **01d-blueprint-scribe**: Synthesizes Code Cartographer artifacts.json (and, if available, a live Neo4j graph from Graph Forge) into docs/agent_output/01-architecture/architecture.md — a Markdown document covering modules, REST API surface, layered type breakdown, class hierarchy and frameworks in play, with Mermaid diagrams. Use when asked to document the architecture, write a markdown overview of the code, or summarize "ideas about the code". ([Udaradg/sample-java-project](https://github.com/Udaradg/sample-java-project/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/01d-blueprint-scribe) · 3 copies)
- **01d-blueprint-scribe**: Generate the architecture overview and function reference from code graph artifacts. Use to document the architecture, REST surface, modules, or code structure. ([Vihanga22365/sample-java-project-with-artifacts](https://github.com/Vihanga22365/sample-java-project-with-artifacts/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/01d-blueprint-scribe) · 2 copies)
- **01d-synthesis**: Transforms raw research data into structured insights through affinity clustering and pattern analysis. Use after data collection to find patterns and key findings. Triggers on: "synthesize research", "analyze interviews", "find patterns", "insight report", "research findings". ([jrbrugger/design-workflow](https://github.com/jrbrugger/design-workflow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-workflow/01-research/01d-synthesis) · ★1)
- **01e-validation**: Quality gate that scores research confidence, checks for bias, and makes go/no-go decisions before design. Use after synthesis to validate research quality. - **02-SCI检索式生成器**: ﻿# 02-SCI检索式生成器\n\n## 目标\n把研究问题转成可在 Web of Science / Scopus / PubMed / Embase / Cochrane 等数据库复用的检索式。\n\n## 前置输入\n- 01 的研究问题、PICOS、关键词\n- 目标数据库清单\n- 起始年份（默认近 10 年）\n- 语言限制（如英文）\n\n## 任务步骤\n1. 概念拆解：把研究问题拆为 2–4 个概念块\n2. 收集每个概念块的同义词、缩写、单复数变体、相关 MeSH/Emtree 词\n3. 每个概念块用 OR 组合，整体用 AND 组合\n4. 按数据库语法转写：\n - PubMed：MeSH + tiab + 运算符\n - WoS：TS= 字段 + NEAR 通配\n - Scopus：TITLE-ABS-KEY + 高级算符\n - Embase：/exp + ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/02-SCI%E6%A3%80%E7%B4%A2%E5%BC%8F%E7%94%9F%E6%88%90%E5%99%A8) · ★1)

- **022__skills_generate-ts-guesses__v13**: To improve the guess quality: 1. Place the migrating hydrogen at the midpoint between the donor and acceptor atoms. 2. Use the reactant geometry with the hydrogen moved; do not optimize the hydrogen position beforehand. 3. If the donor-acceptor distance is large (&gt;3.0 Å), first shrink the distance by moving the acceptor towards the donor (or vice versa) while keeping the hydrogen at the midpoint. ([Jesse-zjx/reaxevolve](https://github.com/Jesse-zjx/reaxevolve/tree/HEAD/evolution/snapshots/022__skills_generate-ts-guesses__v13))
